## Retirement log

<!-- G4 retirement log: one line per retired test, added in the PR that retires it. scripts/test-register.mjs carries it into the register. -->

- 11 Nov 2026, Pilot #1024 (CF-247 F-PDESC prompt-based provider descriptions, Platform Admin 11 Nov 2026 14:57): `cf-247-f-pdesc-run-validate-phone` (3 phone tests) retired with the Run & Validate card it guarded (component `ProviderDescriptionRunValidate`, retiring); the card's browser walk `tests/integration-provider-description/provider-description-postgres.spec.mjs` (4 tests, outside `tests/uat`) and its fixture server went with it. The new design is guarded by `cf-247-pdesc-prompt-based-contract` (12 tests).
- 12 Nov 2026, Pilot (CF-247 F-CDESC cdesc-source-grounded-v1, Platform Admin 11 Nov 2026, Admin PR #430): three tests of `cf-247-s360-course-descriptions-complete` retired with the D4 length re-ask they guarded (course-text.ts `courseTextBreach` and `courseTextReask*`, worker v0.59.10): "the breach is measured as the database measures it, and only a length or sentence-count breach is re-asked", "the re-ask states the limits and the previous text, asks for a rewrite (never a cut) and keeps the deadlines; the note records the first answer" and "worker v0.59.10: the re-ask is gated by the setting (ships 0), uses the same pinned model and page, keeps the first answer on failure and never cuts". The description now has its own call with the pinned prompt `cdesc-source-grounded-v1` (worker v0.72.0, migration 20261112170000; setting `enrich.course_description_reask_max` retiring). The new design is guarded by `cf-247-cdesc-source-grounded-contract` and `tests/fixtures/cdesc-source-grounded/run.sh`.
- 12 Nov 2026, Pilot #1059 (CF-247 F-SCH production redesign, Platform Admin directive 12 Nov 2026 16:12 §6): the test "scraper only: no direct read, no fallback, robots.txt not consulted for scholarship pages; a page waits for the scraper" of `cf-247-s9-scholarship-evidence-journey-contract` retired with the 11 Oct 2026 scraper-only rule it guarded; single scholarship pages are now read directly first (robots.txt respected) and through Firecrawl when the direct copy is not usable evidence (worker v0.77.0). Its still-valid checks (site maps and rendered listings through the scraper, waiting pages tried again, register search retired) moved to a renamed test in the same spec; the new rule is guarded by `cf-247-f-sch-04-extract-v3-contract`.
- 13 Nov 2026, Pilot #1128 (CF-247 Models & services redesign v2.16.0, Platform Admin 13 Nov 2026 19:14 "UI is useless and grown organically ... remove/retire all other older scripts"): `cf-247-f-ops-04-ai-config-phone` (2 phone tests) retired with the AI & OpenRouter screen it measured (`src/AiConfig.jsx`, deleted); the phone acceptance of the page that replaced it is `cf-247-ai-models-services-phone` (2 tests). Three tests of `cf-247-s28-ai-openrouter-tab-contract` retired with the same screen: "nav: Models & services has Services & limits and AI & OpenRouter tabs", "AI & OpenRouter: balance and mode, guards per use, central pages model, scholarship models, profiles" and "Toolsets & limits is part of Services & limits; the AI model switches are on AI & OpenRouter"; the tab, its four panes, the redirects of the retired cards and the role that opens it are guarded by `cf-247-ai-models-services-ui` (12 tests). The spec keeps its test of `admin_ai_config_read`, which the key controls still use. Retired with the same change: "making a qualified model active asks for a reason and leaves AI admission off" of `cf-247-s15-scholarship-ai-extraction-contract` (the Make active button is retired: the reading order is the cascade of the Scholarship pages service); "Models & services › OpenRouter: each AI use against its daily guard, and the credit floor" of `cf-247-s23-ai-limits-journey-contract` (the OpenRouter block of Toolsets is retired). Rewritten, not retired: the model half of `cf-247-models-services-contract` "switch a model off (its steps go too), switch a service on" (now the page-service switch only), `cf-247-s16-scholarship-ai-chain-layer4-contract` "Extraction sets the reading order" (now: shows it read only), the OpenRouter lines of `cf-247-toolsets-limits-contract`, `cf-247-ss020-step3-merged-tabs-contract`, `cf-247-s31-actionable-notices-contract`, `cf-247-s81-menu-review-contract`, `cf-247-s98-ui-audit-phase1-list-internals-contract` and `cf-247-adapters-lifecycle-contract`, which follow the screen to its new place.
- 14 Nov 2026, Pilot (CF-247 S1-AER, AER v2 prompt v3 benchmark on the platform AI evaluation framework, Platform Admin 14 Nov 2026 option 1 twice): `cf-247-aer-v2-eval-set-contract` (3 tests) retired with the design it guarded. It guarded the #1137 set `aer-v2-eval-50-v1` in `pipeline.aer_v2_eval_cases` (38 of its 50 cases had no trusted label) and the proposed plan in `pipeline.aer_v2_eval_plans`; both tables are now retiring. The benchmark is a frozen `pipeline.ai_eval_sets` set of 50 person-decided examples with frozen gold rows, three candidate versions and proposals, scored by `security.ai_eval_score_v1`.
